RUN_MANIFEST {"label":"K_normal/FORM","classification":"FORM","scope":"build_material_carrier","old":"    rows, _, provenance = n.face_factory(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, 'MATERIAL', mu_slot)","replacement":"    original_builder = a.build_material_face_source\n    original_cache = dict(a._FACE_CACHE)\n    def mixed_material_face_source(*args, **kwargs):\n        result = original_builder(*args, **kwargs)\n        components = list(result.normal_exact)\n        components[0] = components[0] + a.grad_W[1]\n        length = sp.sqrt(a.dot(tuple(components), tuple(components)))\n        return n.replace(result, normal_exact=tuple(value / length for value in components))\n    a.build_material_face_source = mixed_material_face_source\n    a._FACE_CACHE.clear()\n    try:\n        rows, _, provenance = n.face_factory(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, 'MATERIAL', mu_slot)\n    finally:\n        a.build_material_face_source = original_builder\n        a._FACE_CACHE.clear()\n        a._FACE_CACHE.update(original_cache)","exact_fragment_count":1,"production_sha256":"7377cd5e062c5bcd73addd98a3bff385a489261807d2876629dcd92b29f7c57a","executed_sha256":"b6f144f75e1af30f500eb6b02726f280f848ef6b6736ed2cc5ff36784f6e41cf","source_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_c50fhnr0/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py","sibling_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_c50fhnr0/scripts","invocation":"/usr/bin/python3 -B /var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_ablation_harness.py --worker /tmp/s11cc2_n6_c50fhnr0/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py /tmp/s11cc2_n6_c50fhnr0 /tmp/s11cc2_n6_c50fhnr0/compact.json","knife_diff":"--- S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:before\n+++ S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:after\n@@ -88,7 +88,22 @@\n def build_material_carrier(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, mu_slot, slots):\n     # A temporary-copy carrier-map ablation belongs inside this function; its\n     # returned velocity is deliberately unused, so it cannot rebind source V.\n-    rows, _, provenance = n.face_factory(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, 'MATERIAL', mu_slot)\n+    original_builder = a.build_material_face_source\n+    original_cache = dict(a._FACE_CACHE)\n+    def mixed_material_face_source(*args, **kwargs):\n+        result = original_builder(*args, **kwargs)\n+        components = list(result.normal_exact)\n+        components[0] = components[0] + a.grad_W[1]\n+        length = sp.sqrt(a.dot(tuple(components), tuple(components)))\n+        return n.replace(result, normal_exact=tuple(value / length for value in components))\n+    a.build_material_face_source = mixed_material_face_source\n+    a._FACE_CACHE.clear()\n+    try:\n+        rows, _, provenance = n.face_factory(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, 'MATERIAL', mu_slot)\n+    finally:\n+        a.build_material_face_source = original_builder\n+        a._FACE_CACHE.clear()\n+        a._FACE_CACHE.update(original_cache)\n     return n.pressure_coefficients(rows, slots), rows, provenance\n \n \n","knife_diff_sha256":"79e0553fccfd8d3654986d66baac38c341d4310755e19ddf547d72c1dc2eee0b"}

RUN_MANIFEST {"label":"K_normal/X2","classification":"COEFFICIENT x2","scope":"build_material_carrier","old":"    rows, _, provenance = n.face_factory(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, 'MATERIAL', mu_slot)","replacement":"    original_builder = a.build_material_face_source\n    original_cache = dict(a._FACE_CACHE)\n    def mixed_material_face_source(*args, **kwargs):\n        result = original_builder(*args, **kwargs)\n        components = list(result.normal_exact)\n        components[0] = 2 * components[0]\n        length = sp.sqrt(a.dot(tuple(components), tuple(components)))\n        return n.replace(result, normal_exact=tuple(value / length for value in components))\n    a.build_material_face_source = mixed_material_face_source\n    a._FACE_CACHE.clear()\n    try:\n        rows, _, provenance = n.face_factory(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, 'MATERIAL', mu_slot)\n    finally:\n        a.build_material_face_source = original_builder\n        a._FACE_CACHE.clear()\n        a._FACE_CACHE.update(original_cache)","exact_fragment_count":1,"production_sha256":"7377cd5e062c5bcd73addd98a3bff385a489261807d2876629dcd92b29f7c57a","executed_sha256":"5d8bb1d8c9ef93cfcb6b272d37ac482fa80ae086b0c82eeed24a94b610ef206b","source_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_3lczt21w/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py","sibling_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_3lczt21w/scripts","invocation":"/usr/bin/python3 -B /var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_ablation_harness.py --worker /tmp/s11cc2_n6_3lczt21w/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py /tmp/s11cc2_n6_3lczt21w /tmp/s11cc2_n6_3lczt21w/compact.json","knife_diff":"--- S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:before\n+++ S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:after\n@@ -88,7 +88,22 @@\n def build_material_carrier(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, mu_slot, slots):\n     # A temporary-copy carrier-map ablation belongs inside this function; its\n     # returned velocity is deliberately unused, so it cannot rebind source V.\n-    rows, _, provenance = n.face_factory(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, 'MATERIAL', mu_slot)\n+    original_builder = a.build_material_face_source\n+    original_cache = dict(a._FACE_CACHE)\n+    def mixed_material_face_source(*args, **kwargs):\n+        result = original_builder(*args, **kwargs)\n+        components = list(result.normal_exact)\n+        components[0] = 2 * components[0]\n+        length = sp.sqrt(a.dot(tuple(components), tuple(components)))\n+        return n.replace(result, normal_exact=tuple(value / length for value in components))\n+    a.build_material_face_source = mixed_material_face_source\n+    a._FACE_CACHE.clear()\n+    try:\n+        rows, _, provenance = n.face_factory(a, b, inputs, alpha, rho, 'MATERIAL', mu_slot)\n+    finally:\n+        a.build_material_face_source = original_builder\n+        a._FACE_CACHE.clear()\n+        a._FACE_CACHE.update(original_cache)\n     return n.pressure_coefficients(rows, slots), rows, provenance\n \n \n","knife_diff_sha256":"c737059eece9d2e30fb59dd6ca361168a55d5074b5f391fe68d609b3284c36c0"}

RUN_MANIFEST {"label":"K_source_route/X2","classification":"COEFFICIENT x2","scope":"run","old":"    source = closed_response(comp, inputs, m_coeff, ds, kernels)","replacement":"    source = closed_response(comp, inputs, m_coeff, {s: {w: 2 * value for w, value in terms.items()} for s, terms in ds.items()}, kernels)","exact_fragment_count":1,"production_sha256":"7377cd5e062c5bcd73addd98a3bff385a489261807d2876629dcd92b29f7c57a","executed_sha256":"73f691975ed72a74c50867866a00888c8927ec0dfb188189d8e372f71035ace9","source_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_5w03gn3j/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py","sibling_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_5w03gn3j/scripts","invocation":"/usr/bin/python3 -B /var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_ablation_harness.py --worker /tmp/s11cc2_n6_5w03gn3j/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py /tmp/s11cc2_n6_5w03gn3j /tmp/s11cc2_n6_5w03gn3j/compact.json","knife_diff":"--- S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:before\n+++ S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:after\n@@ -257,7 +257,7 @@\n     add('SOURCE_BRIDGE_RESIDUAL', n.residual(se, sm))\n     kernels = {s: n.kernel_coefficients(inputs, alpha, rho, s) for s in c.FACES}\n     carrier, _ = n.build_increment(comp, inputs, dc, ms, kernels, slots)\n-    source = closed_response(comp, inputs, m_coeff, ds, kernels)\n+    source = closed_response(comp, inputs, m_coeff, {s: {w: 2 * value for w, value in terms.items()} for s, terms in ds.items()}, kernels)\n     cross = closed_response(comp, inputs, dc, ds, kernels)\n     add('CARRIER_CHANNEL', carrier)\n     add('SOURCE_CHANNEL', source)\n","knife_diff_sha256":"7294f458761dd5cdb767805ec8ed0f3e9a844c048a6107e57f8fb72474c7ea01"}

RUN_MANIFEST {"label":"K_operand_swap/FORM","classification":"FORM","scope":"run","old":"ms[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero)","replacement":"es[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero)","exact_fragment_count":1,"production_sha256":"7377cd5e062c5bcd73addd98a3bff385a489261807d2876629dcd92b29f7c57a","executed_sha256":"4bcf25a8d27488fa655c965024e9809f8e5fc25608d39e1e0577ac74cef7dde2","source_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_iynmj20k/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py","sibling_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_iynmj20k/scripts","invocation":"/usr/bin/python3 -B /var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_ablation_harness.py --worker /tmp/s11cc2_n6_iynmj20k/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py /tmp/s11cc2_n6_iynmj20k /tmp/s11cc2_n6_iynmj20k/compact.json","knife_diff":"--- S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:before\n+++ S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:after\n@@ -208,7 +208,7 @@\n     ms = {s: n.source_terms(inputs, alpha, rho, s, mu_m, m_v[s]) for s in c.FACES}\n     # Differences originate at the two interface operands, never from R_N6.\n     dc = {key: e_coeff[key] - m_coeff[key] for key in e_coeff}\n-    ds = {s: {w: es[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero) - ms[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero)\n+    ds = {s: {w: es[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero) - es[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero)\n               for w in sorted(set(es[s]) | set(ms[s]), key=str)} for s in c.FACES}\n     emit('N6RC_PROVENANCE', {\n         'source_sha256': {p.name: hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in\n","knife_diff_sha256":"beccaa3c52e80f6aa60e63de680b9e0f30860d4305c834157c0907e8da07dfa4"}

RUN_MANIFEST {"label":"K_operand_swap/X2","classification":"COEFFICIENT x2","scope":"run","old":"ms[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero)","replacement":"2 * ms[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero)","exact_fragment_count":1,"production_sha256":"7377cd5e062c5bcd73addd98a3bff385a489261807d2876629dcd92b29f7c57a","executed_sha256":"7ee27120adb8c5bbbad832a6692d55614515cc39e8430f28dbc05dd7a003fe96","source_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_ianzja92/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py","sibling_path":"/tmp/s11cc2_n6_ianzja92/scripts","invocation":"/usr/bin/python3 -B /var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_ablation_harness.py --worker /tmp/s11cc2_n6_ianzja92/scripts/S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py /tmp/s11cc2_n6_ianzja92 /tmp/s11cc2_n6_ianzja92/compact.json","knife_diff":"--- S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:before\n+++ S11c_c2_N6_reconcile_sympy.py:after\n@@ -208,7 +208,7 @@\n     ms = {s: n.source_terms(inputs, alpha, rho, s, mu_m, m_v[s]) for s in c.FACES}\n     # Differences originate at the two interface operands, never from R_N6.\n     dc = {key: e_coeff[key] - m_coeff[key] for key in e_coeff}\n-    ds = {s: {w: es[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero) - ms[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero)\n+    ds = {s: {w: es[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero) - 2 * ms[s].get(w, sp.S.Zero)\n               for w in sorted(set(es[s]) | set(ms[s]), key=str)} for s in c.FACES}\n     emit('N6RC_PROVENANCE', {\n         'source_sha256': {p.name: hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in\n","knife_diff_sha256":"91f869cdb1340fd03d2b0bc8dee3b24860e8582c26c1bbe1e777b7b07d899b80"}
